# Graph Report - proxy-cache  (2026-10-25)

## Corpus Check
- 1758 files · ~2,922,006 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 145 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: .toml 81, (none) 17, .woff2 9)

## Summary
- 31440 nodes · 73530 edges · 1105 communities (976 shown, 129 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 94% EXTRACTED · 6% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 4269 edges (avg confidence: 0.86)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `0a48a035`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
